# UNI2 script and data catalogue

Generated by `tools/squirrel/catalog.py uni2 <data dir>`; do not edit by hand. JSON: `uni2_files.json`, `uni2_names.json`. Translations are machine (`"tr":"machine"`) until reviewed; JP is kept beside them.

## Shared files

| path | Squirrel | load site | timing | hot reload | purpose | header (EN) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `System/PlayBonusTbl.txt` | yes |  | untraced | no | play bonus table | Value added depending on situation |
| `data/BattleInfo.txt` | yes | Boot_LoadGlobalResources -> BattleInfo_LoadGlobal 0x52FD90 | boot | reloadstd | global battle tuning values, g_BattleInfo | Attacker side |
| `data/BoundEff.txt` | yes | Boot_LoadGlobalResources -> BoundEff_LoadGlobal 0x59F790 | boot | reloadstd | wall/ground bounce effect table, g_BoundEffTable | Desc: hitstun effect definition |
| `data/VectorTable.txt` | yes | Boot_LoadGlobalResources 0x8DD0F0 -> VectorTable_LoadGlobal 0x529260 | boot | reloadstd | global vector (knockback/launch) table, g_VectorTable | vectorlist |
| `data/_combase.txt` | yes | Sq_InitBattleVM "_com_base_" | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | CPU AI base classes CCom/CComSkill/CComMove | - Always check that created actions produce no errors / - Tune COM at max difficulty / - Tech is done automatically internally. |
| `script/Battle_Init.txt` | yes | Sq_InitBattleVM 0x8808B0 -> Sq_RunScriptFile 0x8A6CD0 "BattleInit" | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | battle VM init: loads the btl_Define* constants and debug helpers | Debug output related |
| `script/Battle_Std.txt` | yes | Sq_InitBattleVM 0x8808B0 -> Sq_RunScriptFile "BattleScript" | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | battle VM std script: Battle_Std/BtlPl/BtlOb/BtlPt tables, pulls in the btl_* std files | For connection |
| `script/btl_ChrTableFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | MakeMoveTable/MakeCommandTable: builds each character table from the std tables | If it persists while referenced, try cloning first / Nested tables weren't supported, so fixed to a proper deep copy |
| `script/btl_CmdCheck.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | command check helpers (CmdCheck evaluation) | MEMO |
| `script/btl_Define.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | system constants: Def_Sys_* version switches, def_* flags, pattern numbers | 1.40 |
| `script/btl_Define_Chara.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | character numbers (Def_ChrNo_*) and per-character constants | Character number (unused here) |
| `script/btl_Define_CharaNew.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | additional character constants | Empty file |
| `script/btl_Define_NoLocalDebug.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | debug switches for release builds | Debug message output related |
| `script/btl_MvFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | shared move functions (Battle_Std.* used by moves) | _checkFurimuki: 1 if Mv has turnaround processing after input, else 0 / Should probably store left/right regardless of own facing or turnaround, but leaving as is for now |
| `script/btl_MvInitFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | shared move Init helpers | Attack Init common function |
| `script/btl_SEDefine.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from <chr>_se_list.txt | chara load | reloadchara (rebuild only) | sound effect constants (loaded by each character se_list) | SEPrio constant-like values / is local scoped to this file only? |
| `script/btl_StdCommdandTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | GetStdCommandTable: standard commands (dash, jump, guard, system actions) | Std_CommandTable <- {}; / done this way because tired of shallow copies |
| `script/btl_StdMoveTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | GetStdMoveTable: standard moves (stand, walk, guard, hitstun, knockdown, system moves) | Very long function / _ChrNo: specify character number |
| `script/btl_Std_BattleStatusTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | default Battle_Status values shared by characters | Standard battle status table / loaded per character SC |
| `script/btl_debugFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | debug print helpers | debuglog.write_r("debug_log\n"); //just in case, on every startup |
| `script/cmn_SetPosition.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | round start position helpers | Various SetPosition functions |
| `script/sq_stagebgm.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | stage BGM script (stage_bgm.initround) | Desc: stage_bgm.initround() / Set here if battle BGM changes; takes priority over BGM set in battle table / Invalid unless battle has started when called |

### script/btl_Define.txt (1421)

| name | value | EN | JP |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Def_Sys_ReduceShieldOnThrowInput` | `1` | Make shield harder to leak on throw input | 投げ入力のときはシールドが漏れにくくする |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldSuccess_ClearTechMiss` | `1` | Clear throw-tech-fail state upon successful shield | シールドに成功した時点で投げ抜け失敗状態を解除する |
| `Def_Sys_EnableUnTechAbleAnnounce` | `0` | Display of throw-tech-disabled announcement; disabled along with its function due to current bug | 投げ抜け不能アナウンスの表示。現状不具合があるので機能も含めてオフにしている。 |
| `Def_Sys_EnableLowCPSteerEnderCPChange` | `1` | Change Steer Ender properties for low CP (shorter no-tech, changed effects, etc.) | 低CPのステアエンダーの性能を変更する（受け身不能は短く、演出変更など） |
| `Def_Sys_GuardBreakCommandThrowSidouHitHosei` | `1` | Apply guaranteed-damage scaling if a guard-breaking command throw hits as a starter | ガード崩しになるコマ投げが始動でヒットしたら保証補正をかける |
| `Def_Sys_DashThrowVectorLimit` | `1` | Make dash throw momentum harder to carry | ダッシュ投げの慣性は乗りにくくする |
| `Def_Sys_DashAtkAVectorLimit` | `1` | Make dash A momentum harder to carry | ダッシュAの慣性は乗りにくくする |
| `Def_Sys_ThrowHosyoHosei_LimitSteerEnderGRDBonus` | `1` | Limit Steer Ender GRD gain to max 1 block while guaranteed-damage scaling from normal/command throw applies | 通常投げやコマンド投げによる保証補正が乗っているときはステアエンダーによるGRD増加を最大1ブロックに制限 |
| `Def_Sys_NormalChainWhiffPenalty` | `1` | Penalty if normal's follow-up is performed via whiff cancel | 通常技の派生を空振りキャンセルで出したらデメリットを設ける |
| `Def_Sys_FailThrowTechOnUpInput` | `0` | Disable jump grab (if up is held when grabbed, treat as throw tech failure) | ジャングラを不可にする（掴まれたときに方向キーが上に入っていたら投げ抜け失敗にする） |
| `Def_Sys_SmartStear_GRDBonusLimit` | `1` | When CP is extremely low, reduce Smart Steer GRD bonus  0: fixed increase | CPが極端に低いときはスマートステアのGRDボーナスを減らす 0:固定値増加 |
| `Def_Sys_FastRocketeerDamageHoseiLimit` | `6` | Apply scaling to rising jump attacks *scaling applies at or below this frame | のぼりジャンプ攻撃に補正をかける　※このフレーム以下なら補正がかかる |
| `Def_Sys_FastRocketeerMaxJumpFrame` | `20` | Rising jump attack max detection frame | のぼりジャンプ攻撃の最大検知フレーム |
| `Def_Sys_BackDashPunishDamageHosei` | `85` | Scaling applied when getting guaranteed damage from back step (ground throw only) | バクステから確定を取ったときにかける補正値（地上投げのみ対応） |
| `Def_Sys_ExCutinDrawType` | `1` | 0: normal  1: slightly darker (adjusted since white-out is harsh compared to VP/IW) | 0:通常　1:少し暗く（VPとかIWに比べると白飛びがキツいので調整） |
| `Def_Sys_Concentration_AddTS_NoDamage` | `1` | Remove the effect of speeding up Concentration TS during hitstun | やられ中はコンセのTSを早める効果をなくす |
| `Def_Sys_FixMoveEnemyStageCornerBug` | `1` | Fix bug where back-hitting a half-lock move at corner cannot pull opponent to front | 画面端で半ロック技を背面当てしたときに手前にもってこれない不具合を直す |
| `Def_Sys_FixCameraFocusBug_OnHitDuringExCutinMove` | `1` | Fix bug where an EX move that advances during super flash, if hit the instant it darkens, leaves camera focus stuck | 暗転中進むEX技を暗転した瞬間に被弾するとカメラフォーカスがかかったままになる不具合を直す |
| `Def_Sys_EnableReducedGuardStopOnGuardCancelMove` | `1` | Enable handling for moves weak to Guard Thrust & minimum hitstop to allow Guard Thrust | ガースラに弱い技の処理を有効にする＆ガースラが出せる最低ヒットストップ |
| `Def_Sys_ExSkillInitNageMutekiF` | `0` | Throw-invincible frames on EX special startup. 1.10:3 | EX必殺技でかかりの投げ無敵フレーム。1.10:3 |
| `Def_Sys_EXCutinNageMuteki` | `0` | Whether to forcibly grant throw invincibility at EX cut-in timing 0: none 1+: invincible frames 1.10:2 | EXカットインタイミングで強制的に投げ無敵をつけるか 0:付けない 1～:無敵フレーム 1.10:2 |
| `Def_Sys_FixHosyoHoseiDamegeOnly` | `1` | Add damage-only check to guaranteed-damage scaling | 保証ダメージ補正にダメージ時のみのチェックを入れる |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeAssaultEffect` | `1` | Changed Assault effect & made it advance during time stop | アサルトのエフェクトを変更＆時間停止中も進むタイプに変更 |
| `Def_Sys_AddThrowInActiveEffect` | `1` | Add inactive effect for throw approach | 投げでかかり用のインアクティブエフェクトを追加 |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeMaxCVOEffect` | `0` | Change visuals of 200% CVO effect | 200%CVOのエフェクトの見た目を変える |
| `Def_Sys_AirShieldFallMovable` | `1` | Whether to make fall after air Shield actionable | 空中シールド後の落下中を行動可能にするか |
| `Def_Sys_StearEnderNoAttackHitCheckDamage` | `0` | In Steer Ender non-followup check, whether to check MV hit; note: whiff combos cause extreme scaling | ステアエンダーの追撃不能時のチェックで、MVのヒットをチェックするかどうか　※空振りコンボで補正がすごいことになる |
| `Def_Sys_IWE_IncreaseDamageAtkRectSize` | `1` | Enlarge IWE shockwave to hit easier while opponent in hitstun | 相手がやられ中はIWEの衝撃波を大きくしてあたりやすくする |
| `Def_Sys_ChainShift_ResetCharaAirCount` | `1` | Reset air special move count limit on CS activation | CS発動時に空中必殺技回数制限をリセットする |
| `Def_Sys_AirSkillStartupLightLegsMuteki` | `0` | On air special activation, if previous action had Def_HitCheckFlag_LightLegs, continue briefly; caution: large number will bug with other attacks that have attribute invincibility | 空中必殺技発動時、Def_HitCheckFlag_LightLegsが前の行動にあったら少しだけ継続　※大きい数字にして他に属性無敵がある攻撃だとバグるので注意 |
| `Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToShield` | `1` | Treat shield action as counter-hit vulnerable | シールド動作中を被カウンターにする |
| `Def_Sys_EnableDashShieldCommand` | `40` | Allow shield during dash and set dash momentum speed to what percent | ダッシュ中にシールドを出せるようにする＆ダッシュ慣性速度を何％にするか |
| `Def_Sys_TechCommandThrowPunish` | `1` | Make command throws that can be teched untechable if Punish is taken | 投げ抜け可能なコマ投げでパニッシュをとったら投げ抜け不可にする |
| `Def_Sys_SetSpGaugeLimit_Throw` | `0` | Limit gauge gain after ground normal throw and scaling value (large effect) | 地上の通常投げのあとのゲージ回収に制限をかける＆補正値（影響大きめ） |
| `Def_Sys_SetGuardPlus_ForwardShiftDodge` | `0` | After a successful Creeping Edge evade, allow guarding from either side | クリーピングエッジで回避成功後は表裏どっちでもガード可能にする |
| `Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToVeilOff` | `1` | Make Veil Off motion take counter hits | ヴェールオフ動作中を被カウンターにする |
| `Def_Sys_ThrowF_BMCaptureHoseiType` | `0` | 0: gold throw of normal throw always gets scaling  1: no gold-throw scaling when normal throw's gold throw becomes red throw | 0:通常投げの金投げは必ず補正がかかる　1:通常投げの金投げで赤投げになったときに金投げの補正をかけない |
| `Def_Sys_Concentration_CheckEnemyKyoriWithGRDStock` | `1` | Extension of Def_Sys_Concentration_CheckEnemyKyori: reduce effect when GRD is low | Def_Sys_Concentration_CheckEnemyKyori の拡張でGRDが少ないときに影響を減らす |
| `Def_Sys_Concentration_EnemyDamageCheck` | `1` | Reduce long-duration Concé effect while opponent in hitstun | 相手がやられ中の長時間コンセの効果を下げる |
| `Def_Sys_StartHitVO_MinusTime` | `5000` | Reduce VO time slightly if VO hits as starter | VOが始動でヒットしたらVO時間を少し減らす |
| `Def_Sys_GuardShieldThrow_NoGouinNageHosei` | `1` | Do not apply gold throw scaling when grabbing a Guard Shield | ガードシールドを掴んだときは金投げの補正をかけない |
| `Def_Sys_FixUseExSkillSpCost_KoTiming` | `1` | Fix bug where no gauge is consumed if KO'd during EX special startup | EX必殺技のでかかりでKOされたときにゲージ消費をしないバグを直す |
| `Def_Sys_FixSetHosyoHosei_IWtoIWE` | `1` | IFXX grab object | IW>IWは保証補整がかかるが、IW>IWEだと保証補整がかからなかった不具合を直す |
| `Def_Sys_NoPunishSkillGouinNage` | `0` | Don't show Punish display on special-move command throws (disabled to match 1.20 Def_Sys_TechCommandThrowPunish fix) | 必殺技の金投げでパニッシュ表記を出さないようにする（1.20のDef_Sys_TechCommandThrowPunish修正に合わせて無効化 |
| `Def_Sys_EasyDashAtkCommandAfterDash` | `1` | Make dash attack come out via 0606 after dash | ダッシュ攻撃をダッシュの後0606で出るようにする |
| `Def_Sys_AirAtkLandingClearComboLimit` | `1` | Whether landing from air attack resets combo limit | ジャンプ攻撃の着地でコンボリミットを初期化するか |
| `Def_Sys_ChrAttackImpactParticle` | `0` | Perform per-character particle processing at Attack Impact (heavy, so 0 on PS3) | キャラ別のアタックインパクトでのパーティクル処理を行う(重めなのでPS3では0) |
| `Def_Sys_EraseObjHitBackFlags_UniqIDChange` | `1` | Projectiles and AtkObj clear _ObjFlags_ToParentHitBack when creator parent changes | 飛び道具とAtkObjは生成親が変わったタイミングで_ObjFlags_ToParentHitBackを消す |
| `Def_Sys_ComboRateSetStartTiming` | `1` | Change combo rate application from Init to Start timing | コンボレートをかけるのをInitではなくStartタイミングに変更する |
| `Def_Dbg_EXSLimit_EXSkillOki` | `0` | Whether to apply gauge gain limit to okizeme after gaining advantage with EX move | EX技で有利をとっての起き攻めにゲージ増加制限をかけるかどうか |
| `Def_Sys_FireBallSousai` | `1` | Projectile-vs-projectile clash | 飛び道具同士の相殺 |
| `Def_Sys_CelesVorpal` | `1` | Whether to implement Celestial Vorpal | セレスティアル・ヴォーパルを実装するか |
| `Def_Sys_ThrowCounterToForwordShift` | `1` | Whether normal throws make 3D easier to crush | 通常投げで3Dを潰しやすくするか |
| `Def_Sys_Shield_EraseEnemyUniqCancel` | `2` | On shield success, remove opponent's special cancel routes. 1: remove all, 2: remove only those with def_MC7_ActiveEraseEnemyUniqCancel flag | シールド成功時に相手の特殊なキャンセルルートを消す　1:消す　2:def_MC7_ActiveEraseEnemyUniqCancel フラグがあるものだけ消す |
| `Def_Sys_AssultAirAtkCanGuardChainShift` | `1` | Allow CS when an Assault J attack is guarded | アサルトJ攻撃をガードされたときにCS可能にする |
| `Def_Sys_BoundTableUkemiCPBonus` | `1` | Test processing applying CP-based scaling from the 2nd vector table onward | ベクトルのtbl２つ目以降時にCPに応じた補正をかけるテスト処理 |
| `Def_Sys_FixPosAfterThrowTech` | `140` | Fix coordinates after throw tech and specify position; command throws excluded | 投げ抜け後の座標を固定化する＆座標指定　※コマ投げは別 |
| `Def_Sys_DelayedConvertChargeDemerit` | `1` | Performance drops when Concé is done later than opponent | 相手より遅れてコンセしたときに性能が低下する |
| `Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBreak` | `2` | On VO hit, break or strip VP. Up to CLR is 1. 0:no effect 1:CLR 2:strip VP only (raw) | VOヒット時にブレイクしたりVPを剥がしたりする CLRまでは1 0:効果なし　1:CLR　2:生でVP剥がすのみ |
| `Def_Sys_Concentration_SubExs` | `0` | Reduce own EXS during Concentration | コンセ中に自分のEXSを減らす |
| `Def_Sys_Shield_SubExs` | `0` | Reduce own EXS while shielding | シールド中に自分のEXSを減らす |
| `Def_Sys_ThrowF_EnableStick1` | `1` | Throw with 1AD | 1ADで投げを出す |
| `Def_Sys_SmartStear_EnableStick4` | `1` | Allow A chain with 4A | 4AでA連を可能にする |
| `Def_Sys_Concentration_CheckEnemyKyori` | `1` | Reduce effect based on distance to opponent during Concentration | コンセ中に相手との距離を見て効果を下げる |
| `Def_Sys_CVO_AddUkemiTime` | `1` | Increase opponent's unteachable (no-tech) time during CVO | CVO中は相手の受身不能時間を増やす |
| `Def_Sys_Concentration_AddTS` | `5` | Speed up TS rotation during Concentration (gain per frame) | コンセ中にTSの回転を早くする(毎Fの増加値) |
| `Def_Sys_Shield_AddTS` | `250` | Speed up TS rotation during Concentration (gain on success) | コンセ中にTSの回転を早くする(成功時の増加値) |
| `Def_Sys_EXSkillVorpalBonus` | `2` | On EX activation, increase EXS if VP. 2:only while opponent is in hitstun | EX技発動時にVPだとEXSを増やす 2:相手がやられ中のみ有効 |
| `Def_Sys_CmdShortAtkInit` | `10` | Insert SetCommandShortDelay at normal startup F (stopgap for moves dropping when mashing normals) | 通常技のでかかりにSetCommandShortDelayを仕込むF(連打で通常技を出したときに技が漏れやすいのを応急対策) |
| `Def_Sys_ComboLiberate_BoundHosei` | `0` | Whether to apply scaling on CVO bound  1: UNIclr and earlier, with scaling | CVOのバウンドで補正をかけるかどうか　1:UNIclrまで・補正あり |
| `Def_Sys_VeilOff_SubEnemyGRDStock` | `1` | Landing VO reduces opponent's GRD | VOをあてると相手のGRDをへらす |
| `Def_Sys_CheckObjectEnemyGuardMuki` | `1` | Remove front/back sides of placed objects etc.; needs per-move setting | 設置物などの表裏をなくす　※技毎に個別設定が必要 |
| `Def_Sys_EnableExtendHoldForever` | `1` | Release button mid-charge invalidates charge attacks (treat Def_JFIDNHB_HoldForever as set) | タメ攻撃は途中でボタンを離したら全て無効にする(Def_JFIDNHB_HoldForeverをあることにする) |
| `Def_Sys_NoPlayTrainingRestartUpsetEffect` | `1` | Suppress post-restart presentation in training mode | トレモでのリスタート直後の演出を抑える |
| `Def_Sys_NoCmdCheckDuringAnten` | `1` | Block commands from CmdCheck during super flash | 暗転中はCmdCheckでコマンドが出ないように弾く |
| `Def_Sys_NewTrapYarareCheck` | `3` | Unify placed-object hitstun checks with new handling; 2: removable by projectiles, 3: removable by light attacks (= no more unremovable) | 設置のやられチェックを新しい処理で揃える 2:飛び道具でも消せる 3:弱攻撃でも消せる（＝消せなくするのをなくす） |
| `Def_Sys_ChainShiftGRDLimit` | `12` | 6: at most 6 CS (up to clr)  12: effectively unlimited | 6:最大6個のCSにする（clrまで） 12:実質無制限 |
| `Def_Sys_EnableCrossupBarrier` | `1` | Make Shield come out with 6D against cross-up J attacks | めくりJ攻撃に対して6Dでシールドが出るようにする |
| `Def_Sys_EasierLaunchAirAssult` | `1` | Make low-air Assault easier to perform and adjust startup frames | 低空アサルトを出しやすくする＆発生フレーム調整をする |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeGRDAddValue_OverheadFrame` | `1` | Change GRD gain on mid guard according to startup frames | 発生フレームに応じて中段ガード時のGRD増加量を変更する |
| `Def_Sys_FF_RecoverGRD` | `0` | Restore GRD via FF effect 0or1 CLR:1 (except Mercava FF) | FFの効果でGRDを回復する 0or1 CLR:1　※メルカヴァFFのみ除外 |
| `Def_Sys_FastSkillVecPosShift` | `1` | Whether to add anti-tunneling handling for specials with fast vectors | 早いベクトルのある必殺技ですり抜けないような処理をいれるかどうか |
| `Def_Sys_CVO_ResetAnyLimit` | `1` | On CVO, reset some usage-count limits (currently only jump cancel) | CVO時に使用回数制限系をいくつかリセットする(現状ジャンプキャンセルのみ) |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldHoldCommandCheckStrict` | `1` | Stricter check so shield-hold input is less likely to be dropped | シールドホールドコマンドが漏れにくくチェックを厳密に |
| `Def_Sys_ForwardShiftMinusDelay` | `0` | Whether recovery is reduced if cancelled in second half of 3D | 3Dの後半で相殺したら硬直が減るかどうか |

| `Def_Sys_ThrowF_BreakTimePar` | `20` | Rate at which break time is reduced when hit by normal throw during Break; intended to reduce per-character differences from throw animation length | ブレイク中に通常投げを食らった時、ブレイク時間を減らす割合。これを入れると、投げの演出の長さによるキャラ差が減る想定 |
| `Def_Sys_MutekiHosei` | `50` | Overwrite scaling applied on MV exit after an invincible move whose announcement finished hit as starter (safety handling) | 無敵アナウンスを出し終えた技が始動でヒットしたあとのMV抜け時にかける上書き補正（保険処理的なもの） |
| `Def_Sys_MutekiHosyouHosei` | `1` | Whether to limit guaranteed damage when invincible move is starter; applies def_HosyoHosei_Muteki_Init scaling | 無敵技始動で保証ダメージに制限をかけるかどうか def_HosyoHosei_Muteki_Init の補正がかかる |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeMutekiHosyoTiming` | `1` | Change timing of setting guaranteed-damage limit for invincible starter; 0:AttackImpact 1:LastUpdate for Skill only | 無敵技始動の保証ダメージ制限をセットするタイミングを変える　0:AttackImpact 1:SkillだけはLastUpdate |
| `Def_Sys_GRDIncreaseAnyCancelSkill` | `0` | Increase GRD gain on guard for specials with many cancel routes | キャンセルルートの多い必殺技ガード時のGRD増加量を増やす |
| `Def_Sys_RapidAtkA_Hosei` | `0` | Whether to apply scaling when A attack is done via rapid-cancel | 連キャンでA攻撃を出したときに補正をかけるか |
| `Def_Sys_ThrowCounterTechMissWaitType` | `1` | 0: red throw presentation same as gold throw (CLR)  1: same as normal throw | 0:赤投げの演出は金投げと同じ(CLR)　1:通常投げと同じ |
| `Def_Sys_AnnounceAtemiFailed` | `1` | Whether to show announcement on hit during parry | 当身中の被弾でアナウンスを出すかどうか |
| `Def_Sys_EnemyAntenStopNoCancel` | `1` | Make some actions uncancelable when darkened; make Assault uncancelable if red haze appears on CS hit | 被暗転時に一部アクションをキャンセル不可にする＆被CSで赤いもやが出たらアサルトをキャンセル不可にする |
| `Def_Sys_VorpalCutinPosType` | `1` | 0: same position as VORPAL announce  1: depends on character facing (same as cut-in) | 0:VORPALアナウンスと同じ位置　1:キャラの向き依存（カットインと同じ） |

| `Def_Sys_IWEGRDBreakType` | `1` | 0: Break or Vorpal ends on activation  1: consumed only if it did not hit | 0:発動でブレイクorヴォーパル解除　1:消費はヒットしなかったときだけ |
